Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the precipitation structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 597. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 574 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_597(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 597. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^574(S, F) is trivial. QED.
